# Digest 20261009-053536 (2026-10-09)

**Searches run:** sclc, thoracic-other (new searches, on-demand backfill `--since 2026-01-01`).

**Errors:** none. All sources returned `ok`. **Truncation:** the sclc ClinicalTrials.gov registrations query (cond "small cell lung cancer") is flagged `truncated`: 601 records matched but only 500 were retrieved (the max_results cap), so about 100 SCLC registrations or updates from this window were not checked. A later run in smaller date chunks can pick them up. That query also brings in many NSCLC-only trials, rated low here as off-topic for SCLC. No web queries.

**Counts:**
- sclc: PubMed, window 2026/01/01 to 2026/10/09: 1154 found, 813 new
- sclc: ClinicalTrials.gov registrations/updates (cond "small cell lung cancer"), window 2026-01-01 to 2026-10-09: 601 found, 324 new **truncated**
- sclc: ClinicalTrials.gov results-posted (cond "small cell lung cancer"), window 2026-01-01 to 2026-10-09: 108 found, 78 new
- thoracic-other: PubMed, window 2026/01/01 to 2026/10/09: 1079 found, 1039 new
- thoracic-other: ClinicalTrials.gov registrations/updates (cond "mesothelioma"), window 2026-01-01 to 2026-10-09: 17 found, 15 new
- thoracic-other: ClinicalTrials.gov registrations/updates (cond "thymoma OR thymic carcinoma"), window 2026-01-01 to 2026-10-09: 13 found, 12 new

Total new: 2274 (0 practice-changing, 27 high, 276 medium, 1971 low). 7 items match both searches.

Backfill run: medium reasons are one line, and low items have no reason.

## Practice-changing

None this run. Nothing from Jan-Oct 2026 in these two fields meets the bar. CheckMate 743's 5-year update confirms a standard that is already in place. The NCCN and STS mesothelioma documents do not say in their abstracts which recommendation changed. The FDA tarlatamab and PMDA atezolizumab items are journal profiles of approvals. The rest are registrations or registry postings.

## High

### sclc: publications
- **NIPINEC (GCO-001): nivolumab ± ipilimumab in pretreated advanced neuroendocrine carcinoma.** In this non-comparative randomised phase 2, 185 patients with platinum-refractory gastroenteropancreatic NEC or lung large-cell NEC (half lung primaries) received nivolumab or nivolumab-ipilimumab in the second or third line. The 8-week ORR was 7.2% (95% CI 2.7–15.1) with nivolumab and 14.0% (7.4–23.1) with the combination (best ORR 9.6% vs 20.9%). Median PFS was about 2 months and OS about 6 months in both arms. The authors call the combination a possible later-line option, but the benefit is small, the arms were not formally compared, and the lung cohort is LCNEC rather than SCLC. Walter TP, Mazieres J, Otto J et al. *J Clin Oncol* 2026. [PMID 41774886](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41774886/)
- **FDA Approval Summary: tarlatamab for ES-SCLC.** The FDA review behind the May 2024 accelerated approval for ES-SCLC that progressed on or after platinum. It rests on DeLLphi-301 (n=99 at the approved dose; ORR 40% [95% CI 31–51], median DoR 9.7 months). The label carries a boxed warning for CRS and neurotoxicity including ICANS. This is a profile of a 2024 decision, not a new regulatory action. Liu Y, Nashed J, Barone AK et al. *Clin Cancer Res* 2026. [PMID 41587109](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41587109/)
- **IASLC Pathology Committee: grade 3 lung carcinoids/NETs as a new diagnostic category.** The study collected 28 resected tumours with carcinoid morphology but >10 mitoses/2 mm² from 9 institutions; WHO currently calls these LCNEC. All had wild-type Rb/p53, wild-type RB1/TP53 (n=9 sequenced), low TMB and carcinoid-type genomics. OS and RFS were worse than for typical (n=117) and atypical (n=52) carcinoids. The committee proposes classifying them as grade 3 carcinoids/NETs, with Ki-67 as an added criterion, so that treatment follows carcinoid rather than LCNEC pathways. It is a proposal from a small cohort; WHO has not yet adopted it. Rekhtman N, Hanna MG, Hofman P et al. *J Thorac Oncol* 2026. [PMID 42790640](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42790640/)
- **Tarlatamab in pulmonary carcinoid.** A single-centre (MSK) series of 11 patients with DLL3-high metastatic pulmonary carcinoid treated with tarlatamab. ORR was 73% (8/11), disease control 100%, and median PFS was not reached. CRS occurred in 82% in cycle 1, 2 of them grade 3. This is the first sign that DLL3 T-cell engagers work in carcinoids, a disease with few active drugs, but the series is tiny, retrospective, selected for high DLL3 and has short follow-up. Ross JS, Lee J, Ortiz E et al. *J Thorac Oncol* 2026. [PMID 42562260](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42562260/)
- **DLL3 IHC predicts response and outcome with tarlatamab (preprint, not peer reviewed).** In 54 MD Anderson patients with SCLC treated with tarlatamab, DLL3 >75% by CLIA IHC was linked to longer time on treatment (10.1 vs 2.9 months, p=0.036) and a trend to longer OS (not reached vs 9.5 months, p=0.066). Median DLL3 was 80% in partial responders and 40% in progressors (p=0.006). DLL3 did not track intracranial response. Expression varied across 138 profiled patients (median 70%). This could support biomarker selection for DLL3-directed drugs, but it is retrospective, single-centre and not yet reviewed. Zhang B, Hong L, Wang K et al. *bioRxiv* 2026. [PMID 42818314](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42818314/)

### sclc: results posted
- **DeLLphi-304: tarlatamab vs chemotherapy in relapsed SCLC (NCT05740566).** Amgen posted results to the registry for the phase 3 (n=509) that compared tarlatamab with investigator's-choice lurbinectedin, topotecan or amrubicin. Primary endpoint OS: median 13.6 months (95% CI 11.1–NE) vs 8.3 (7.0–10.2), HR 0.60 (0.47–0.77), p<0.001. These are registry results; the posting adds no peer-reviewed report. [NCT05740566](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05740566)
- **BMS-986012 (anti-fucosyl-GM1) added to carboplatin-etoposide-nivolumab, first-line ES-SCLC (NCT04702880).** BMS posted results for this randomised phase 2 (n=135). PFS by blinded central review was 5.78 vs 5.59 months, HR 0.83 (95% CI 0.55–1.25), p=0.37, so the add-on did not clearly help. Deaths were 47 vs 53 by data cut-off, and serious AEs 38 vs 34. These are registry results without a peer-reviewed report. [NCT04702880](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04702880)

### sclc: new trial registrations
Registrations are dominated by phase 3 trials that replace first-line platinum-etoposide with ADCs or T-cell engagers.
- **ZL-1310 (DLL3 ADC) + atezolizumab or durvalumab vs platinum-etoposide + checkpoint inhibitor (incl. atezolizumab + lurbinectedin maintenance), first-line ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, not yet recruiting, n=530, Zai Lab. [NCT07796100](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07796100)
- **BrenDeLL-Lung01: BL-M14D1 (DLL3 ADC) + atezolizumab vs carboplatin-etoposide-atezolizumab followed by atezolizumab + lurbinectedin, first-line ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, recruiting, n=550, SystImmune. [NCT07625644](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07625644)
- **PANKU-Lung07: BL-B01D1 (EGFR×HER3 bispecific ADC) + tislelizumab vs platinum-etoposide + tislelizumab, first-line ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, recruiting, n=562, Sichuan Baili. [NCT07502300](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07502300)
- **YL201 (B7-H3 ADC) + serplulimab vs carboplatin-etoposide + serplulimab, treatment-naive ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, recruiting, n=442, MediLink. [NCT07739758](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07739758)
- **QLC5508 + QL2107 vs tislelizumab + platinum-etoposide, treatment-naive ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, not yet recruiting, n=556, Qilu. [NCT07802821](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07802821)
- **DAREON-Lung-1: obrixtamig (DLL3×CD3 T-cell engager) + atezolizumab-carboplatin-etoposide vs standard chemo-immunotherapy, first-line ES-SCLC.** Phase 3, recruiting, n=670, Boehringer Ingelheim. [NCT07472517](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07472517)
- **Tarlatamab + durvalumab vs durvalumab alone after chemoradiotherapy, LS-SCLC.** Phase 3, not yet recruiting, n=430, PrECOG. Tests whether adding tarlatamab improves on ADRIATIC consolidation. [NCT07637513](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07637513)
- **Subcutaneous vs intravenous tarlatamab, ES-SCLC after platinum.** Phase 3, recruiting, n=400, Amgen. PK non-inferiority primary endpoint. [NCT07778316](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07778316)

### thoracic-other: publications
- **CheckMate 743 five-year update: first-line nivolumab + ipilimumab vs platinum-pemetrexed in unresectable pleural mesothelioma.** At a median follow-up of 66.8 months, OS benefit persisted: 5-year OS 14% vs 6%, HR 0.74 (95% CI 0.62–0.88). Benefit held across histologies and after adjusting for the 24% chemotherapy-arm crossover to immunotherapy (HR 0.64, 0.53–0.78). No new safety signals were seen. High baseline monocytic MDSCs were linked to worse OS on the combination (HR 1.25), an exploratory finding. This confirms an existing standard and is not a new one. Scherpereel A, Baas P, Nowak AK et al. *J Clin Oncol* 2026. [PMID 41734361](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41734361/)
- **NEMO (EORTC-08112-LCG): switch-maintenance nintedanib vs placebo after platinum-pemetrexed, pleural mesothelioma.** The triple-blind randomised phase 2 closed early for poor accrual after LUME-Meso reported negative (n=37). Descriptively, nintedanib was worse: median PFS 3.4 vs 4.6 months (HR 2.25, 1.07–4.73) and OS 13.1 vs 38.9 months (HR 2.38, 1.05–5.43). More placebo patients went on to immunotherapy (87% vs 54%). This is a negative, underpowered trial from the pre-immunotherapy era that closes the antiangiogenic maintenance question. Abdel-Rahman O, Taylor P, O'Brien M et al. *Lung Cancer* 2026. [PMID 41539214](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41539214/)
- **Gemcitabine maintenance vs best supportive care after first-line platinum chemotherapy, diffuse pleural mesothelioma.** This single-centre (Egypt), open-label randomised phase 2 enrolled 64 patients. Median PFS was 6.2 vs 2.8 months (reported HR 4.33, 95% CI 2.48–7.56, p<0.001) with no OS difference (p=0.155). There was more haematological toxicity and no grade 3–4 events. It is small and open-label, and its BSC comparator predates first-line immunotherapy, so it has limited relevance where nivolumab-ipilimumab is standard. Sobeih ME, Helal M, Yahia M et al. *BMC Cancer* 2026. [PMID 41888696](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41888696/)
- **NIBIT-EPI-MESO: tumour DNA-methylation subtypes predict immunotherapy outcome in pleural mesothelioma.** Multi-omics of pre-treatment tumours from 91 ICI-treated patients (earlier trials and routine care) defined four methylation subsets: demethylated, LOW, intermediate and CIMP. The LOW subset was enriched for responders, had the longest OS and a T- and B-cell-rich microenvironment. CIMP was enriched for non-responders with the shortest OS and an immune-depleted microenvironment. The authors built a methylation-based classifier for ICI outcome. It is a promising first predictive biomarker in this disease, but it is retrospective with a modest n and needs prospective validation. Calabrò L, Caruso FP, Covre A et al. *Nat Genet* 2026. [PMID 42045690](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42045690/)
- **ARTIMES: AI-assisted volumetric response criteria for pleural mesothelioma.** Built from 10,926 CT scans of 2,080 patients in 14 cohorts and validated in 8 trials (4,674 scans, 943 patients). ARTIMES outperformed mRECIST for prognosis (C-index 0.83 vs 0.73, p=0.023) and detected progression a median 5 weeks earlier. At trial level, ARTIMES-based PFS correlated with OS (R² 88%) where mRECIST did not (R² 6%). This could become the response standard for mesothelioma trials, but the study is retrospective and needs prospective validation. Lipman KBWG, Wittenberg R, de Oliveira Taveira M et al. *Lancet Oncol* 2026. [PMID 42309108](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42309108/)
- **STS 2026 Expert Consensus on multimodal treatment of pleural mesothelioma.** An international panel answered 13 PICO questions by modified Delphi (≥75% agreement). Diagnosis needs adequate pleural biopsy plus at least CT and PET. Decisions belong in a tumour board with expert thoracic surgeons. Surgery should only be part of multimodal care, and (extended) pleurectomy/decortication is strongly favoured over extrapleural pneumonectomy. It is relevant after MARS2, but the abstract does not say which earlier recommendation changed, and it is a surgical society consensus. Velotta JB, Roden AC, Rice J et al. *Ann Thorac Surg* 2026. [PMID 42019659](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42019659/)
- **NCCN Guidelines: Mesothelioma: Pleural, version 3.2026.** The JNCCN publication of the current NCCN pleural mesothelioma guideline. The abstract only says it covers evaluation and treatment and does not list what changed in this version, so check the "Updates" section of the full guideline before relying on any particular change. Stevenson J, Riely GJ, Wood DE et al. *J Natl Compr Canc Netw* 2026. [PMID 42607715](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42607715/)
- **PMDA regulatory update incl. atezolizumab for thymic carcinoma in Japan.** An editorial summarising recent Japanese approvals, one of which is atezolizumab for thymic carcinoma. There is no abstract, so the approval date, label wording and supporting trial are not given here. It is a journal profile of an approval in a new indication for an existing drug, not the regulatory action itself. Matsumura N. *Int J Clin Oncol* 2026. [PMID 41504991](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41504991/)
- **Ivonescimab + paclitaxel-carboplatin, first-line advanced thymic carcinoma (preliminary phase 2).** In a single-centre phase 2, the first 7 patients with stage IVb thymic carcinoma had ORR 85.7% (6/7; 95% CI 42.1–99.6) and DCR 100% at a median 6.4 months. Grade ≥3 treatment-related AEs occurred in 57% (mostly neutropenia) and grade ≥3 irAEs in 14%. These are the first data for a PD-1×VEGF bispecific in thymic carcinoma, but with n=7 and short follow-up they are hypothesis-generating. Ma JT, Sun L, Zhang LY et al. *J Immunother Cancer* 2026. [PMID 42778290](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42778290/)
- **Surgery followed by entire-hemithoracic RT for thymoma with pleural dissemination (prospective phase 2).** 71 patients (32 de novo, 39 recurrent) had macroscopic resection then 14 Gy/14 fractions to the hemithorax, plus a mediastinal boost for >T2. At a median of 57 months, median PFS was 50 months, 5-year PFS 50.8% and 5-year in-field relapse-free survival 66.5%. 5-year OS was 98.6% and toxicity mostly mild. Results are promising for stage IVA thymoma, but the trial is single-arm with no comparator, and the best dose and volume are open. Wang C, Mao T, Gu Z et al. *Int J Radiat Oncol Biol Phys* 2026. [PMID 42632550](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42632550/)

### thoracic-other: new trial registrations
- **VT3989 (TEAD palmitoylation inhibitor) vs investigator's-choice gemcitabine or vinorelbine, previously treated advanced epithelioid pleural mesothelioma.** Phase 3, not yet recruiting, n=350, Vivace Therapeutics. The first registrational trial of a Hippo/TEAD-targeted drug. [NCT07824986](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07824986)
- **Lucitanib (VEGFR/FGFR inhibitor) 15 mg or 10 mg vs placebo, advanced thymic carcinoma after chemotherapy.** Randomised phase 2a/2b, completed, n=74, Haihe Biopharma. No results are posted yet. [NCT07824505](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07824505)

## Medium

### sclc
- [[Expert consensus on multidisciplinary diagnosis and treatment of small cell carcinoma of the esophagus (2026 edition)]](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41813228/): Chinese consensus on small cell carcinoma of the oesophagus, extrapulmonary.
- [Neoadjuvant immunochemotherapy in potentially resectable small cell lung cancer: a real-world retrospective analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808721/): Retrospective neoadjuvant immunochemotherapy in resectable SCLC.
- [Induction immunochemotherapy before definitive chemoradiotherapy versus definitive chemoradiotherapy alone in limited-stage small-cell lung cancer: a multicenter propensity score-matched analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41808131/): Multicentre PSM: induction chemo-IO before CRT in LS-SCLC improved PFS (retrospective).
- [Locus-Specific Human Endogenous Retrovirus ERVK18 Expression Indicates an Inflamed Microenvironment and Favorable Immunotherapy Outcome in Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41784525/): ERVK18 expression as predictive biomarker for chemo-IO in SCLC, validated in trial cohort.
- [Real-world comparative effectiveness of atezolizumab versus durvalumab for extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41780087/): Real-world atezolizumab vs durvalumab regimens in ES-SCLC: equivalent outcomes.
- [Multiomic characterization of small cell lung cancer: Real-world insights into therapeutic opportunities](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41761743/): Large real-world multiomic SCLC profiling linking subtypes to immune signatures and targets.
- [Immunotherapy and the Sequence Relative to Survival Outcomes in SCLC: Analysis of the National Cancer Database](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41749824/): NCDB analysis of IO timing relative to CRT in SCLC (retrospective).
- [Outcomes of Concurrent Radiotherapy With Tarlatamab in Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer From the DLL3 PanTUMOR Database](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41748376/): Multicentre real-world: concurrent radiotherapy with tarlatamab appears safe in ES-SCLC.
- [Early Experience with Tarlatamab (T-Cell Engagers) for Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer (ES-SCLC) in Canada: Lessons Learned and Implementation Strategies](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41744848/): Canadian implementation experience of tarlatamab outpatient delivery.
- [Immunohistochemical profiling of small cell lung cancer reveals subtype heterogeneity and a tuft cell-like subtype characterized by LRMP and TRPM5](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41739157/): IHC subtyping of SCLC identifying a tuft-cell-like POU2F3/LRMP/TRPM5 subset.
- [Eukaryote initiation factor 6 modulates small-cell lung carcinoma plasticity via the integrin-FAK signaling axis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41735293/): eIF6-integrin-FAK axis drives SCLC plasticity and chemoresistance, in vivo data (Nat Commun).
- [Orally Bioavailable Cyclin A/B RxL Inhibitors: Optimization of a Novel Class of Macrocyclic Peptides That Target E2F-High and G1-S-Checkpoint-Compromised Cancers](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41721763/): Oral cyclin A/B RxL inhibitor with SCLC xenograft regressions, now in phase 1.
- [Treatment of lung neuroendocrine tumors with 177Lu-DOTATATE: Experience from a Tertiary Oncology Center](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41720218/): Single-centre 177Lu-DOTATATE PRRT outcomes in lung carcinoids.
- [Timing, fractionation, and dose of thoracic radiotherapy in patients with extensive-stage small cell lung cancer undergoing first-line Chemo-Immunotherapy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41704721/): Retrospective timing/dose of thoracic RT with first-line chemo-IO in ES-SCLC.
- [Utility of RB1 immunohistochemistry for prognostic subtyping of pulmonary large cell neuroendocrine carcinoma](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41699273/): RB1 IHC loss as prognostic marker in advanced LCNEC.
- [Safety evaluation of tarlatamab: A pharmacovigilance study based on the FAERS database](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41679015/): FAERS pharmacovigilance analysis of tarlatamab adverse events.
- [Phase I study of amrubicin plus cisplatin and concurrent accelerated hyperfractionated thoracic radiotherapy for limited-stage small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41671905/): Phase 1 amrubicin-cisplatin with accelerated hyperfractionated TRT in LS-SCLC; 5-year OS 64.8%.
- [Targeting NHEJ activates STING signaling through MYC degradation to boost antitumor immunity in SCLC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41667501/): DNA-PKcs inhibition activates STING via MYC degradation, boosting IO in SCLC models (Nat Commun).
- [Continuing Immunotherapy Beyond Progression Prolongs the Survival of Patients With Extensive-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A Multicenter Retrospective Analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41656161/): Multicentre retrospective: IO beyond progression in ES-SCLC.
- [Clinical impact of first-line chemotherapy combined with immune checkpoint inhibitors for limited-stage small cell lung cancer patients: a real-world propensity score matching study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41646844/): Real-world PSM: ICI added to first-line therapy in LS-SCLC.
- [Next-generation anti-DLL3 radiopharmaceuticals targeting high-grade neuroendocrine lung and prostate cancers](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41628333/): Next-generation 177Lu anti-DLL3 radiopharmaceuticals improve survival in SCLC models (PNAS).
- [Impact of immunotherapy on survival and treatment patterns of patients with extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: a study using a Japanese nationwide database](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41612977/): Japanese nationwide database: ICI uptake and survival in ES-SCLC.
- [Post-marketing safety surveillance of tarlatamab: a real-world pharmacovigilance study based on the FAERS database](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41609856/): FAERS pharmacovigilance analysis of tarlatamab.
- [Paving the Way for CCK2R-Targeted Peptide Receptor Radionuclide Therapy with [177Lu]Lu-DOTA-MGS5 in Patients with Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41599245/): CCK2R-targeted 177Lu-DOTA-MGS5 PRRT feasibility in SCLC patients.
- [Optimal Treatment Strategies for Pulmonary Large Cell Neuroendocrine Carcinoma Based on Molecular Subtypes](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41598557/): Molecular subtype-guided chemotherapy choice in LCNEC (retrospective).
- [ISL1: A Novel Neuroendocrine Subtype in Small Cell Lung Cancer Predicts Durable Response to Lurbinectedin](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41582485/): ISL1-defined SCLC subtype predicting lurbinectedin sensitivity (preclinical/translational).
- [Characterization of POU2F3-expressing large cell neuroendocrine carcinoma of the lung: A comprehensive analysis of morphology, immunohistochemistry, and genomic alterations](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41581412/): POU2F3-expressing LCNEC characterised as a distinct subgroup.
- [Everolimus as maintenance therapy in advanced neuroendocrine neoplasms: results from the MAVERIC phase II trial](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41572426/): Randomised phase 2 MAVERIC: everolimus maintenance in advanced NEN, benefit mainly GEP-NEN.
- [Spatial transcriptomics reveals molecular heterogeneity and subtype-specific therapeutic targets in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41545500/): Spatial transcriptomics of SCLC subtype heterogeneity and targets.
- [Optimizing Tarlatamab Delivery in Small Cell and Neuroendocrine Lung Cancer: Real-World Insights into Step-Up Dosing and Prophylactic Tocilizumab Use](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41544604/): Real-world tarlatamab: higher CRS/ICANS than trials; prophylactic tocilizumab may mitigate.
- [Lung carcinoids (lung neuroendocrine tumors): integrated classification, molecular biology, diagnosis, and multidisciplinary management](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41526992/): Comprehensive Endocrine Reviews overview of lung carcinoid classification and management.
- [HDAC6 orchestrates metastatic and immunosuppressive programs in small cell lung cancer through S100A2-TGF-β/SMAD and CSF1R signaling](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41501879/): HDAC6 drives metastatic and immunosuppressive programmes in SCLC; dual inhibition in vivo (Mol Cancer).
- [Analysis of SCLC subtype markers (ASCL1, NEUROD1, POU2F3, YAP1), DLL3, OTP, and TTF1 in 300 lung carcinoids and enteropancreatic neuroendocrine tumours](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41489019/): Subtype markers and DLL3 across 300 lung carcinoids and EP-NETs; DLL3 targeting implications.
- [Tarlatamab plus anti-PD-L1 as first-line maintenance for ES-SCLC demonstrates promising results](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41482887/): News item on tarlatamab plus anti-PD-L1 first-line maintenance in ES-SCLC.
- [Three-Tier Prognostic Stratification of Lung Carcinoids (NET G1-G2-G3) by Multivariable, Data-Driven Integration of Ki-67 and Mitotic Count](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42165962/): Three-tier Ki-67/mitosis prognostic grading of resected lung carcinoids.
- [Epigenomic profiling of neuroendocrine lung cancers identifies a classical-neuroendocrine ASCL1/NKX2-1 subtype and a SOX11-associated differentiation axis linked to reduced immunogenicity](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42160949/): Epigenomic subtyping of neuroendocrine lung cancers, SOX11 axis linked to low immunogenicity.
- [Intracranial efficacy of combined immune checkpoint inhibitors and platinum plus etoposide therapy in patients with extensive-disease small-cell lung cancer and brain metastases](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42155337/): Intracranial efficacy of first-line chemo-ICI in ES-SCLC brain metastases (retrospective).
- [Immunotherapy retreatment with serplulimab vs. chemotherapy in extensive-stage advanced small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42145910/): Retrospective serplulimab retreatment vs chemotherapy in ES-SCLC.
- [Consolidation thoracic radiotherapy after chemoimmunotherapy provides survival benefit in elderly patients with extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: a multicenter study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42125428/): Multicentre retrospective: consolidative TRT after chemo-IO in elderly ES-SCLC.
- [Incidence, Risk Factors, and Impact of Immunotherapy on Brain Metastasis in Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer Without Baseline Brain Involvement](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42119045/): Brain metastasis incidence in ES-SCLC on chemo-IO without baseline BM; IO does not reduce it.
- [HDAC Inhibition Unlocks Tumor Plasticity and Enhances Immunotherapy Response in Myc-Driven Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42068133/): Entinostat reprograms NE state and enhances ICB in Myc-driven SCLC allografts.
- [Association of tumor CD73 expression with clinical outcomes in small cell lung cancer treated with first-line chemo-immunotherapy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42061276/): Tumour CD73 expression associated with chemo-IO outcomes in SCLC.
- [Elective nodal irradiation of high-risk regions is superior to involved field radiotherapy for limited-stage small cell lung cancer: a propensity score-matched retrospective study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42050661/): PSM retrospective: elective nodal irradiation vs IFRT in LS-SCLC.
- [Real-World Clinical Outcomes of Concurrent Chemotherapy and Dose-Escalated Twice-Daily Radiotherapy With Simultaneous Integrated Boost (SIB) for Limited-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42036274/): Real-world dose-escalated BID RT with SIB in LS-SCLC.
- [Real-World Experience of Tarlatamab in Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Carcinoma Showed Promising Systemic and Intracranial Efficacy With Higher Neurologic Toxicity](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42024839/): Real-world tarlatamab: systemic and intracranial efficacy, higher ICANS than in trials.
- [Consolidative Radiotherapy for Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer: Outcomes Based on Completeness of Consolidation and Oligometastatic State](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42019269/): Retrospective consolidative RT completeness in ES-SCLC.
- [Long-Term Response and Survival in Extensive-Stage SCLC Receiving Atezolizumab Plus Chemotherapy in a Real-World Setting: IFCT-1905 CLINATEZO](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42005788/): IFCT-1905 CLINATEZO: real-world atezolizumab-chemo in ES-SCLC reproduces trial survival.
- [Single-cell and spatial transcriptomics reveal that the CXCL12-CXCR4 axis drives the immune-desert phenotype in small cell lung cancer by recruiting immunosuppressive CXCR4+ neutrophils and S100A8+ monocytes](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41991241/): Single-cell/spatial study: CXCL12-CXCR4 axis drives immune-desert SCLC (JITC).
- [HSR26-207: Real-World Survival and Toxicity Outcomes of Tarlatamab in Relapsed or Refractory Small-Cell Lung Cancer: A Multicenter TriNetX Analysis Across Academic and Non-Academic Centers](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41991036/): Multicentre TriNetX real-world tarlatamab outcomes (abstract only).
- [Local Control Outcomes of Patients With Brain Metastases From Small Cell Lung Cancer Treated With Stereotactic Radiosurgery](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41972495/): SRS local control for SCLC brain metastases incl. post-PCI/WBRT recurrences.
- [Spatial multi-omics unveils the monoclonal origin, neuroendocrine plasticity, and microenvironment niches in combined small-cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41966692/): Spatial multi-omics of combined SCLC: monoclonal origin and plasticity (Cell Rep Med).
- [Differential KEAP1/NRF2 mediated signaling widens the therapeutic window of redox-targeting drugs in SCLC therapy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41965332/): TXNRD1 inhibitors with NRF2 activation widen therapeutic window in SCLC models (Nat Commun).
- [POU2F3 in Small Cell Lung Cancer (SCLC): Diagnostic Utility in Neuroendocrine-Low/Negative SCLC and Discrimination From Other Thoracic Malignancies and Other Small Blue Round Cell Tumors](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41956344/): POU2F3 IHC diagnostic utility in NE-low SCLC.
- [BMX inhibition overcomes small cell lung cancer chemoresistance by stabilizing E2F1 via ERK1/2-Cyclin D1/CDK4/6 axis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41946708/): BMX inhibition overcomes SCLC chemoresistance via E2F1, in vivo (STTT).
- [Evidence for the transformation of a subset of pulmonary carcinoids to small cell lung carcinomas through shared molecular alterations](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41939453/): Molecular evidence that a subset of atypical carcinoids transforms to SCLC.
- [Surgery versus chemoradiotherapy in patients with stage T1-2N0M0 small cell lung cancer: a retrospective cohort study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41933341/): Retrospective surgery vs CRT in T1-2N0 SCLC.
- [Treatment monitoring by biomarker analysis in a Phase I dose-expansion study of AZD2811 for relapsed/refractory small-cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41933194/): Phase 1 expansion of AZD2811 (aurora B) in relapsed SCLC with biomarker monitoring.
- [Real-world multi-institution analysis of tarlatamab in patients with small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41930543/): Multi-institution real-world tarlatamab incl. trial-ineligible patients; more ICANS.
- [The lesser the better? A systematic review and meta-analysis of resection strategy in lung neuroendocrine tumors](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41922295/): First meta-analysis of sublobar vs lobectomy for lung NETs: no clear OS difference.
- [Single-cell spatial transcriptomics reveals tumor microenvironment heterogeneity in primary and lymph node-metastatic small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41916294/): Single-cell spatial map of primary vs nodal-metastatic SCLC (Cell Rep Med).
- [Evaluating the safety and efficacy of chemotherapy in patients with relapsed small cell lung cancer combined with allopurinol and MycoPhenolate (CLAMP)](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41904847/): Phase 1 CLAMP: irinotecan + mycophenolate + allopurinol in relapsed SCLC, increased toxicity.
- [Brief Report: Treatment Patterns and Outcomes in a Large Real-World Cohort of Patients With ES-SCLC Treated With First-Line Chemoimmunotherapy in the United States](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41904053/): Large US real-world cohort of first-line chemo-IO in ES-SCLC: poor outcomes, low subsequent therapy.
- [Molecular subtyping of small-cell lung cancer in clinical practice: Primary tumor-lymph node concordance, intratumoral heterogeneity, and genomic context in a Chinese cohort](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41887131/): SCLC IHC subtyping: primary-nodal discordance and intratumoral heterogeneity.
- [LSD1 inhibitor, TAS1440, disrupts INSM1-LSD1 complex activating tumor-suppressive pathways via transcriptional reprogramming in neuroendocrine SCLC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41881985/): LSD1 inhibitor TAS1440 disrupts INSM1-LSD1 in SCLC-A models (Nat Commun).
- [Risk factors for CRS and ICANS with tarlatamab in small cell lung cancer and associated efficacy outcomes: Insights from clinical practice](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41880872/): Multicentre real-world risk factors for tarlatamab CRS/ICANS and efficacy (EJC).
- [Synthetic lethality between RB-loss and E2F3 inhibition in small cell cancers targeted by pyrimidine synthesis blockade](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41860961/): RB-loss/E2F3 synthetic lethality targetable by pyrimidine synthesis blockade (PNAS).
- [Brief Report: Multi-Institution Real-World Analysis Evaluating Safety, Efficacy, and ctDNA Dynamics After Tarlatamab in Patients With Previously Treated SCLC and LCNEC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41847155/): Multi-institution real-world tarlatamab in SCLC and LCNEC with ctDNA dynamics.
- [Multi-omics reveals key molecular and cellular features of advanced small cell lung cancers associated with distinct therapeutic opportunities](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41845530/): Multi-omics of advanced SCLC linking methylation subtypes to therapeutic opportunities (Genome Med).
- [Comparison of 6 cycles with 4 cycles of chemotherapy and atezolizumab in the first-line treatment of ES-SCLC: a retrospective multicenter analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41838398/): Retrospective 6 vs 4 cycles chemo-atezolizumab in ES-SCLC: no PFS/OS gain.
- [Impact of hepatic radiotherapy on survival and safety outcomes in small cell lung cancer patients with liver metastases: a retrospective study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42523939/): Retrospective: liver-directed RT in SCLC with liver metastases.
- [Association of salvage radiotherapy with survival outcomes in extracranial oligoprogression after first-line therapy for extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: a multicenter retrospective cohort study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42504316/): Multicentre retrospective: salvage RT for extracranial oligoprogression in ES-SCLC.
- [Clinicopathological and prognostic validation of pulmonary carcinoid/NET G3: evidence for a distinct subgroup of well-differentiated neuroendocrine neoplasms](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42503268/): Validation of pulmonary carcinoid/NET G3 as a distinct subgroup.
- [Prophylactic Cranial Irradiation and Survival in Limited-Stage SCLC: A Multicenter Real-World Analysis From the Canadian SCLC Database (CASCADE)](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42502471/): Canadian CASCADE database: PCI associated with improved OS in LS-SCLC.
- [Poor Performance Status Associated With Inferior Tarlatamab Outcomes in a Large, Multi-institution Real-World Database of Patients With 2L + ES-SCLC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42501611/): Large real-world tarlatamab database: ECOG PS ≥2 linked to inferior outcomes.
- [Personalized tumor-loaded monocyte-derived dendritic cell vaccination in combination with atezolizumab as maintenance treatment in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer (ES-SCLC): phase Ib-II VENEZOLUNG trial](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498486/): Phase Ib-II VENEZOLUNG: DC vaccine + atezolizumab maintenance in ES-SCLC, small single-arm.
- [Analysis of SLFN11 expression in small cell lung cancer, carcinoids and large cell neuroendocrine carcinoma: insights into lung neuroendocrine neoplasms](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42470748/): SLFN11 expression across lung NENs, multicentre.
- [ABL kinase inactivation induces transcription-replication conflicts and impairs replication fork progression in metastatic small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42461725/): ABL inactivation plus DDR inhibition impairs SCLC metastatic outgrowth in vivo (Cell Rep).
- [Comparative Effectiveness and Safety of Prophylactic Cranial Irradiation Versus Magnetic Resonance Imaging Surveillance in Limited-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42425226/): Comparative effectiveness PCI vs MRI surveillance in LS-SCLC: fewer BM, no OS benefit.
- [Loss of the USP22 deubiquitylase confers resistance to chemotherapy in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42414273/): USP22 loss confers chemoresistance in SCLC; GLUT1 inhibition re-sensitises (Nat Commun).
- [High Endothelial Venules in Small Cell Lung Cancer: Prognostic Subtypes and Therapeutic Implications for Immunoradiotherapy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42400206/): High endothelial venules in SCLC as prognostic subtype marker with immunoradiotherapy implications.
- [Prognostic impact of the 9th vs 8th TNM classification in small-cell lung cancer. a population-based study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42385494/): Population-based validation of TNM 9th vs 8th edition in SCLC.
- [CD68+ tumor-associated macrophages exhibit prognostic value in surgically resected small cell lung cancer: a retrospective cohort study of 614 patients](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42371090/): CD68+ TAMs prognostic in 614 resected SCLC.
- [Prognostic stratification in resected pulmonary neuroendocrine carcinomas: Analysis of the French database EPITHOR](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42341696/): French EPITHOR database: outcomes of resected pulmonary neuroendocrine carcinomas.
- [First-line serplulimab plus chemotherapy versus chemotherapy alone in small-cell lung cancer patients with brain metastases: a multicenter, prospective cohort study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42338583/): Prospective cohort: serplulimab-chemo vs chemo in SCLC with brain metastases.
- [CRISPR screen identifies autophagy inhibition (GNS561) as a PARP inhibitor (AZD5305) combination strategy in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42337800/): CRISPR screen: autophagy inhibitor GNS561 plus PARP1 inhibitor AZD5305 in SCLC.
- [TRIM21-mediated ubiquitination of PARP1 regulated by the PI3K/AKT-STAT5A axis suppresses small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42315836/): TRIM21-PARP1 axis in SCLC with dual inhibition in organoids/xenografts (Nat Commun).
- [Clinical Characteristics and Survival Outcomes in Patients With Small Cell Lung Cancer in Spain: An Analysis of the Thoracic Tumor Registry (2016-2025)](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42315416/): Spanish thoracic tumour registry SCLC outcomes by smoking and sex 2016-2025.
- [Surgery versus radiotherapy after neoadjuvant immunochemotherapy in limited-stage small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42306666/): Retrospective surgery vs RT after neoadjuvant chemo-IO in LS-SCLC.
- [Inducible T Cell Costimulator Ligand and Inducible T Cell Costimulator Stratification Identify Dichotomous Tumor Microenvironment and Guide Chemo-Immunotherapy in Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42253933/): ICOSL/ICOS stratification of SCLC TME guiding chemo-IO benefit.
- [Stereotactic radiosurgery (SRS) for small cell lung cancer (SCLC) brain metastases: a real-world UK tertiary center experience](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42236269/): UK real-world SRS for SCLC brain metastases, comparable to FIRE-SCLC.
- [Updates in Small Cell Lung Cancer Treatment: No Longer Too Small to Ignore-A Review of Recent Therapeutic Advances](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42228883/): ASCO Educational Book review of recent SCLC therapeutic advances.
- [Hormone secretion predicts poor prognosis in lung neuroendocrine neoplasms](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42227353/): Hormone secretion predicts poor prognosis in lung NENs; recommends routine testing.
- [Real-World Survival Over 25 Years in Extensive-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer: The Impact of Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42227304/): 25-year real-world ES-SCLC survival: ICI exposure associated with improved OS.
- [Comparison of protein-based and genetic prognostic markers in a large cohort of primary pulmonary and extrapulmonary small cell neuroendocrine carcinoma](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42217423/): Large cohort comparing pulmonary vs extrapulmonary small cell NEC biology and prognosis.
- [Characteristics and clinical outcomes of a nationwide cohort of patients with small cell lung cancer following first-line treatment: Real-world evidence](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42217422/): Nationwide real-world SCLC outcomes after first-line treatment.
- [IAR025, a novel anti-DLL3 x CD3-bispecific antibody, displays high antitumor efficacy in preclinical models of small-cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42189233/): IAR025, a new DLL3×CD3 bispecific with preclinical in vivo and primate data.
- [Real-World Data of Second-Line Amrubicin Monotherapy in Patients With Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer Who Received First-Line Chemoimmunotherapy or Chemotherapy: A Multicenter Retrospective Study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42174384/): Multicentre real-world second-line amrubicin after chemo-IO in ES-SCLC.
- [Consolidative thoracic radiotherapy after first-line chemoimmunotherapy in extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer: a multicenter retrospective study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42170285/): Multicentre retrospective: consolidative TRT after chemo-IO in ES-SCLC.
- [Clinical characteristics and survival of small cell lung cancer with limited or extensive synchronous metastatic spread - A German population-based cancer-registry based cohort study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42167027/): German registry: limited synchronous metastatic SCLC has prognostic value; supports SCLC-specific OMD definition.
- [Second-line therapy in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: an unmet need highlighted by real-world evidence from the LALUCA registry](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42826612/): LALUCA registry: poor second-line outcomes in ES-SCLC.
- [Plasma nucleosome profiling reports on tumor burden and molecular subtypes in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42820295/): Plasma nucleosome (cfChIP) profiling reports tumour burden and subtypes in SCLC (JCI).
- [Concomitant medications and outcomes of bispecific T-cell engager therapy: A propensity-matched study of tarlatamab in ES-SCLC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42804884/): Propensity-matched: concomitant medications and tarlatamab outcomes in ES-SCLC.
- [The circulating tumor microenvironment in small-cell lung cancer: Vimentin-positive circulating tumor endothelial cells as drivers of metastasis and poor prognosis in a large cohort study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42800661/): Vimentin+ circulating tumour endothelial cells drive SCLC metastasis and prognosis, large cohort.
- [Circadian timing of first-line chemoimmunotherapy is associated with survival in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42788839/): Circadian timing of first-line chemo-IO associated with survival in ES-SCLC (retrospective).
- [Hepatocyte nuclear factor 4 alpha defines a distinct subtype of lung carcinoid with therapeutic potential targeting somatostatin receptor 2](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42782345/): HNF4α defines a distinct lung carcinoid subtype with SSTR2 as target.
- [SCRMP-mediated SUMOylation of CSNK2B facilitates DNA damage repair to promote cisplatin resistance in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42777084/): SCRMP-mediated SUMOylation drives cisplatin resistance in SCLC (Sci Transl Med).
- [Tambotatug pelitecan - a potential new second-line treatment option for SCLC](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42773163/): Nat Rev Clin Oncol highlight: tambotatug pelitecan as potential second-line SCLC option (no abstract).
- [XPO1-mediated TRIM21 nuclear export reprograms TREM2+ macrophage polarization by targeting IRF3 to augment anti-PD-1 efficacy in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42768009/): XPO1 inhibition reprogrammes TREM2+ macrophages to augment anti-PD-1 in SCLC models (Nat Commun).
- [OTP, CD44, Ki-67 Immunohistochemistry to Predict Prognosis in Preoperative Lung Neuroendocrine Tumor Biopsy Specimens](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42767405/): OTP/CD44/Ki-67 IHC on biopsies predicts lung NET prognosis; may guide sublobar resection.
- [Efficacy and safety of omitting the clinical target volume in limited-stage small cell lung cancer: a large-scale multicenter real-world study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42763051/): Large multicentre: omitting CTV in LS-SCLC RT, similar efficacy, less toxicity.
- [Rechallenge With Immunotherapy for ES-SCLC: Efficacy and Safety From a Retrospective Propensity Score-Matched Study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42760803/): PSM retrospective: IO rechallenge in ES-SCLC shows no survival benefit, more irAEs.
- [SCLC TumorMiner: A genomics platform for small cell lung cancer precision oncology](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42733745/): SCLC TumorMiner genomics platform with response biomarkers (iScience).
- [The efficacy and feasibility of neoadjuvant immunochemotherapy versus chemotherapy in Limited-Stage Small-Cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42727356/): Retrospective neoadjuvant chemo-IO vs chemo in LS-SCLC: higher pCR/MPR.
- [Adjuvant chemotherapy and survival after resection for pulmonary large-cell neuroendocrine carcinoma: a dual-center time-dependent analysis](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42723785/): Dual-centre: adjuvant chemotherapy after resected LCNEC associated with survival in stage II-III.
- [T-cell engagers in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42723523/): Cancer review of DLL3 T-cell engagers in SCLC incl. real-world experience.
- [A stem-like chromatin program in small-cell lung cancer is associated with poor outcomes after chemoimmunotherapy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42721975/): Stem-like chromatin programme in SCLC linked to poor chemo-IO outcomes (Cell Rep Med).
- [Treatment-free interval as a determinant of second-line chemotherapy outcomes in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: a Dutch population-based study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42721617/): Dutch population-based: treatment-free interval determines second-line outcomes in ES-SCLC.
- [The Role of Resection for Stage II Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42719153/): NCDB: surgery associated with survival benefit in stage II SCLC.
- [A targetable dependency on nonsense-mediated decay for cellular homeostasis and immune control in small cell lung cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42711693/): NMD dependency as targetable vulnerability enhancing IO in SCLC models (Mol Cancer).
- [European lung cancer congress 2026 - the thoracic radiation oncologist's view](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42699135/): ELCC 2026 radiation oncology highlights, incl. thoracic RT.
- [Evaluating Immunotherapy Outcomes in Elderly Patients with Extensive-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer (KCSG LU23-15)](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42660517/): KCSG LU23-15: atezolizumab benefit attenuated in ES-SCLC patients ≥75 years.
- [Spindle Assembly Checkpoint Competency Determines Sensitivity to KIF18A Inhibition in Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42647139/): SAC competency determines KIF18A inhibitor sensitivity in SCLC models.
- [De Novo Actionable Genomic Alterations in High-Grade Pulmonary Neuroendocrine Carcinomas: Therapeutic Implications of Targeted Treatment](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42646625/): Multicentre: de novo actionable alterations in high-grade pulmonary NEC, targeted therapy implications.
- [Proteomic Profiling Identifies CLDN3 as a Tumor-Selective Therapeutic Target in Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42619739/) (preprint, not peer reviewed): Preprint: CLDN3 identified as tumour-selective SCLC target with in vivo regression.
- [Treatment-stratified associations of pathological complete response with survival in resected limited-stage small cell lung cancer: A multicenter retrospective study with exploratory spatial immune profiling](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42612508/): Multicentre: pCR and survival in resected LS-SCLC by treatment group.
- [Metabolic Tumor Volume as a Predictor of Benefit From Prophylactic Cranial Irradiation in Limited-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42594954/): Multicentre: metabolic tumour volume predicts PCI benefit in LS-SCLC (JNCCN).
- [Proteogenomic characterization of pulmonary large-cell neuroendocrine carcinoma identifies molecular features and therapeutic strategy](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42585338/): Proteogenomic characterisation of LCNEC identifying immunoagonist therapy strategy (Sci Adv).
- [Multiplexed quantitative proteomics in small-cell lung cancer: stage-linked DLL3 distribution and an independent prognostic signal of thymidine phosphorylase](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42582771/): Targeted proteomics: stage-linked DLL3 distribution and TYMP prognostic signal in SCLC.
- [Second-line chemotherapy efficacy is not affected by prior exposure to immunotherapy in extensive-disease small-cell lung cancer: results from the ESME French national real-world cohort](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42567006/): ESME French cohort: prior IO does not affect second-line chemo efficacy in ES-SCLC (ESMO Open).
- [Real-world treatment patterns and outcomes of chemo-immunotherapy with or without radiotherapy in extensive-stage small cell lung cancer: the durvalumab expanded access cohort](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42564214/): Durvalumab expanded-access cohort: chemo-IO ± RT in ES-SCLC, real-world.
- [Real-World Data on Once or Twice-Daily Radiation Therapy and the Effect of Age in Patients With Limited-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42556587/): Real-world BID vs QD RT in LS-SCLC: OS benefit of BID in younger patients only (IJROBP).
- [Trends in Surgical Management of Early-stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42556222/): Trends in surgery for early-stage SCLC: increasing and associated with survival.
- [A Study of DXC006 With Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors or Platinum for Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07559929): Phase Ib/II DXC006 (ADC) with toripalimab or platinum in SCLC (n=200).
- [A Study of YL201 in Combination With Other Anti-Cancer Therapies in Patients With Advanced Solid Tumors](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07407933): Phase Ib/II YL201 (B7-H3 ADC) + atezolizumab, incl. untreated ES-SCLC (n=118).
- [A Prospective, Multicenter Exploratory Clinical Study on Consolidation Therapy With Tislelizumab Combined With Nintedanib for Limited-stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07485920): Single-arm (n=20) tislelizumab + nintedanib consolidation in LS-SCLC.
- [Teniposide, Cisplatin and Serplulimab for Treatment of ES-SCLC, a Randomized Controlled Study](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07583511): Randomised phase 2 (n=90): teniposide vs etoposide with cisplatin + serplulimab in ES-SCLC.
- [Lurbinectedin Plus Paclitaxel Versus Paclitaxel in Patients With Previously Treated Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07640932): Randomised phase 2 (n=69): lurbinectedin + paclitaxel vs paclitaxel in previously treated SCLC.
- [A Prospective, Single-Arm, Single-Center Phase II Study Evaluating the Efficacy and Safety of Chidamide Combined With PD-L1 Inhibitor, Carboplatin, and Etoposide as First-Line Treatment in Patients With Extensive-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer (ES-SCLC)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07373964): Single-arm phase 2 (n=36): chidamide + PD-L1 inhibitor + EC first-line ES-SCLC.
- [QL1706 Plus Anlotinib as a Later-line Treatment for Patients With Advanced Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07636226): Randomised phase 2/3 (n=120): QL1706 + anlotinib as later-line SCLC treatment.
- [A Phase II Clinical Study of SHR-7787 Injection Combined With SHR-1316 Injection in Patients With LS-SCLC](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07798947): Phase 2 (n=120, Hengrui): SHR-7787 + adebrelimab in LS-SCLC.
- [Adebrelimab Maintenance Therapy for LS-SCLC Post Induction Chemo-Adebrelimab Plus CRT or CRT Alone](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07381543): Phase 2 (n=76): adebrelimab maintenance after induction chemo-IO + CRT in LS-SCLC.
- [A 2-part Phase 1/2 Open-label Trial on ODM-212](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07563738): Phase 1/2 ODM-212 with mesothelioma cohort (incl. ipilimumab-nivolumab combination), n=229.
- [Exploration of Treatment Strategies After Concurrent Chemoradiotherapy for LS-SCLC Guided by MRD](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07357532): Phase 2 (n=44): MRD-guided post-CRT strategy with adebrelimab ± apatinib in LS-SCLC.
- [Study of Sacituzumab Govitecan With Atezolizumab/Durvalumab as Maintenance Therapy for Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07339059): Phase 2 (n=35): sacituzumab govitecan + atezolizumab/durvalumab maintenance in ES-SCLC.
- [Medical Access Program for Ifinatamab Deruxtecan in 3L+ Pretreated Extensive-stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07535359): Expanded access programme for ifinatamab deruxtecan in 3L+ ES-SCLC (no longer available).
- [TLN-499 in Combination With Venetoclax in Patients With Relapsed/Refractory Solid Tumors](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07713732): Phase 1 (n=120): TLN-499 + venetoclax in relapsed SCLC, NEC and mesothelioma.
- [Phase 1/2 Multicenter, Open-label, Dose-escalation Study of IDP- 121 in Patients With Platinum-resistant Small Cell Lung Cancer (MYCrocytic)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07864207): Phase 1/2 IDP-121 (MYC-targeted) in platinum-resistant SCLC.
- [A Study of BL-M14D1 in Combination With Atezolizumab in Patients With Extensive-stage Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07654400): Phase 2 (n=36): BL-M14D1 + atezolizumab in ES-SCLC.
- [Sacituzumab Tirumotecan in Combination With Anlotinib in Previously Treated Patients With Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer (ES-SCLC)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07501169): Single-arm phase 2 (n=33): sac-TMT + anlotinib in pretreated ES-SCLC.
- [Symbiotic-Lung-14: A Study to Learn About the Study Medicine Called PF08634404 in Combination With Chemotherapy in Adult Participants With Transformed Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07476287): Phase 2 Symbiotic-Lung-14 (n=40, Pfizer): PF-08634404 (PD-1×VEGF) + chemo in transformed SCLC.
- [Tarlatamab for the Treatment of Extensive Stage Small-cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07423585): Phase 2 (n=39): tarlatamab in ES-SCLC with biospecimen correlates.
- [Study to Confirm Efficacy and Feasibility of Tarlatamab Treatment in Patients With Extensive Stage Small-cell Lung Cancer With Poor Performance Status](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07803848): Phase 2 SPACE-T (n=57, AIO): tarlatamab in ES-SCLC with poor performance status.
- [A Multicenter, Open-label, Randomized, Prospective Clinical Study of Adebrelimab Consolidation Therapy for Limited-Stage Small-Cell Lung Cancer (LS-SCLC) Without Progression After Concurrent Chemoradiotherapy](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07559786): Randomised phase 2 (n=200): adebrelimab consolidation after CCRT in LS-SCLC, residual-field vs involved-field RT.
- [Safety and Preliminary Activity of BI115 in Advanced SCLC](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07559019): Phase 1 (n=150, Innovent): IBI115 in advanced SCLC.
- [A Study to Assess the Safety and Tolerability of BMS-986525 Alone and in Combination Therapy in Participants With Relapsed/Refractory Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07325136): Phase 1/2 (n=240, BMS): BMS-986525 alone or with pumitamig in relapsed/refractory SCLC.
- [A Study of Lurbinectedin in Combination With Durvalumab for the Treatment of Participants With ES-SCLC](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07459634): Phase 2 (n=50, Jazz): lurbinectedin + durvalumab in ES-SCLC.
- [A Study of Alisertib and Paclitaxel in Patients With Small Cell Lung Cancer (SCLC)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07465757): Phase 2 (n=50, Puma): alisertib + paclitaxel in SCLC.
- [A Phase I Study to Evaluate the Safety and Preliminary Efficacy of [225Ac]Ac-DOTATATE Injection Combined With Tislelizumab in the Maintenance Treatment Period for Patients of Extensive-stage Small Cell Lung Cancer (ES-SCLC) With Somatostatin Receptors (SSTR)+ as First-line Treatment](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07413042): Phase 1 (n=24): 225Ac-DOTATATE + tislelizumab maintenance in SSTR+ ES-SCLC.
- [Anlotinib Plus Whole-Brain Radiotherapy for Brain Metastases in SCLC Patients](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07443397): Randomised phase 3 (n=134, CAMS): anlotinib + WBRT for SCLC brain metastases.
- [DAREON®-36: A Study to Test Obrixtamig in Combination With ZL-1310 in People With Advanced Small Cell Lung Cancer or Other Neuroendocrine Cancers](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07707895): Phase 1/2 DAREON-36 (n=60, BI): obrixtamig + ZL-1310 ± atezolizumab in SCLC and NEC.
- [Tarlatamab for SCLC Brain Metastases](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07402343): Single-arm phase 2 (n=35, Maastricht): intracranial efficacy of tarlatamab in active SCLC brain metastases.
- [Study of Tarlatamab + ZL-1310 +/- Anti-programmed Death Ligand 1 (Anti-PD-L1) in Small Cell Lung Cancer (SCLC)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07531095): Phase 1 (n=160, Amgen): tarlatamab + ZL-1310 ± durvalumab in SCLC.
- [A Study of Armored CAR T Cells in People With Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07783477): Phase 1 (n=16, MSK): armoured DLL3 CAR-T (IL-18) in DLL3+ SCLC/NEC.
- [A Phase I Clinical Study to Evaluate the Safety, Tolerability, and Pharmacokinetics of HLX3901 in Patients With Advanced SCLC or NEC](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07416695): Phase 1 (n=138, Henlius): HLX3901 in advanced SCLC/NEC.
- [A Multicenter Phase II Trial to Evaluate Rechallenge With Platinum-based Chemotherapy (Carboplatin, Etoposide) for Progression During Tarlatamab Therapy in Relapsed Extensive Disease Small-cell Lung Cancer (SCLC)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07699237): Phase 2 (n=54, Cologne): carboplatin-etoposide rechallenge on progression during tarlatamab in relapsed SCLC.
- [Study Evaluating Tarlatamab in Chinese Participants With Advanced Small Cell Lung Cancer After Two or More Prior Lines of Treatment](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06502977) (results posted): Results posted: tarlatamab phase 2 in Chinese patients with ES-SCLC after ≥2 lines (n=32, Amgen).
- [Testing the Addition of an Anti-cancer Drug, BAY 1895344, to Usual Chemotherapy for Advanced Stage Solid Tumors, With a Specific Focus on Patients With Small Cell Lung Cancer, Poorly Differentiated Neuroendocrine Cancer, and Pancreatic Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04514497) (results posted): Results posted: phase 1 elimusertib (ATR) + chemotherapy with SCLC/NEC focus (NCI).
- [A Phase 1/2 Trial of TC-510 In Patients With Advanced Mesothelin-Expressing Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05451849) (results posted): Results posted: terminated phase 1/2 of TC-510 (mesothelin-targeted engineered T cells) in mesothelioma (n=14).
- [Stereotactic Radiation in Patients With Small Cell Lung Cancer and 1-10 Brain Metastases](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT03391362) (results posted): Results posted: SRS for SCLC with 1-10 brain metastases (n=100).
- [Efficacy and Safety of Pembrolizumab Plus Investigational Agents in Combination With Chemotherapy as First-Line Treatment in Extensive-Stage Small Cell Lung Cancer (ES-SCLC) (MK-3475-B99/ KEYNOTE-B99)](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04924101) (results posted): Results posted: KEYNOTE-B99 phase 2 umbrella, pembrolizumab + investigational agents + chemo first-line ES-SCLC (n=122).
- [Pembrolizumab and Concurrent Chemoradiotherapy or Radiation Therapy in Treating Patients With Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT02402920) (results posted): Results posted: phase 1 pembrolizumab with chemoradiotherapy/RT in SCLC (MD Anderson, n=83).

- [Iparomlimab Plus Tovorilimab Combined With Bevacizumab and Chemotherapy as First-Line Treatment for Advanced Mesothelioma](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07514793): Single-arm phase 2 (n=37): iparomlimab-tuvonralimab + bevacizumab + chemo first-line mesothelioma.
- [A Study of BMS-986504 With Standard-of-Care Therapy for People With Solid Tumor Cancer](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07532902): Phase 1 (n=60, MSK): BMS-986504 with SoC incl. ipilimumab-nivolumab in MTAP-deleted mesothelioma.
- [A Phase II, Investigator-Initiated Exploratory Study of Sacituzumab Tirumotecan for Previously Treated Unresectable Thymic Carcinomas](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07750353): Phase 2 (n=35, NCC Japan): sac-TMT in previously treated thymic carcinoma.
- [6 vs 3 Cycles of Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy for Potentially Resectable Locally Advanced Thymic Epithelial Tumors](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07463313): Randomised phase 2/3 (n=116): 6 vs 3 cycles neoadjuvant chemo in locally advanced TETs.
- [Sacituzumab Tirumotecan in Participants With Locally Advanced or Metastatic Thymic Carcinoma](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07324629): Phase 2 (n=30, Kelun): sac-TMT in advanced thymic carcinoma.
- [Neoadjuvant Adebrelimab Plus CRT for Locally Advanced Thymic Carcinoma](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07479628): Phase 2 (n=31): neoadjuvant adebrelimab + CRT in locally advanced thymic carcinoma.
